TL;DR: 本文提出维度到组合来源归属（Dimension-to-Composition Source Attribution）框架，将混合来源文本检测从二元判断转化为推断内容来源和表达来源后组合为四类协作类型的问题，并设计了D2C-Routing系统，在MixD2C基准上达到0.8603的四路Avg TPR@1%FPR，比同切分的RACE本地重跑高6.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成文本检测通常被框架为二元文档级判断（人类撰写或机器生成），但这一框架在混合来源写作场景下失效——内容来源（content origin）和表达来源（expression origin）可能不同。需要新的范式来处理这类混合来源文本的检测问题。

Method: 提出Dimension-to-Composition Routing（D2C-Routing）方法：先分别路由内容侧和表达侧的证据到有监督的维度头（dimension heads），再通过一个可学习的门控组合层（gated composition layer）预测最终标签。该方法将混合来源检测重新定义为维度到组合的来源归属任务，推断内容来源和表达来源后组合成四类协作类型。

Result: 在MixD2C（由HART混合来源基准重构的切分）上，所披露的D2C-Routing检测系统达到0.8603的四路Avg TPR@1%FPR，比同切分的RACE本地重跑高出6.5个点。核心消融实验支持路由设计，错误分析显示区分"AI内容/人类表达"与"完全AI生成"文本仍是最困难的边界。

Conclusion: D2C-Routing有效解决了混合来源文本检测问题，验证了将内容来源与表达来源分别推断再组合的框架可行性。最困难的分类边界在于AI内容但人工表达与完全AI生成之间的区分，这为后续研究指出了改进方向。

Abstract: AI-generated text detection is commonly framed as a binary document-level judgment about whether a text is human-written or machine-generated. This framing breaks down for mixed-origin writing, where content origin and expression origin may differ. We cast mixed-origin detection as dimension-to-composition source attribution, inferring content origin and expression origin before composing them into four collaboration types. We propose Dimension-to-Composition Routing (D2C-Routing), which routes content-side and expression-side evidence to supervised dimension heads before a learned gated composition layer predicts the final label. On MixD2C, a reconstructed split derived from the HART mixed-origin benchmark, our disclosed D2C-Routing-based detector system reaches 0.8603 four-way Avg TPR@1%FPR, 6.5 points above the same-split RACE-local rerun. Core ablations support the routing design, while error analysis shows that distinguishing AI-content/human-expression from fully AI-generated text remains the hardest boundary. Code is available at https://github.com/bystander563/d2c-routing-artifact.

</details>


### [205] [RATIO: A Benchmark for Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations in Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27394)
*Maayan Sharon,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RATIO（Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations），一个大规模科学文献检索基准，通过三种"构思操作"（Address/寻址、Broaden/泛化、Specify/具体化）定义相关性，构建自百万级CS全文论文语料，为文学启发型检索提供训练与评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献检索不仅可以直接提供问题解决方案，还能在不同抽象层次上激发灵感（更一般或更具体的视角）。现有工作缺乏一个大规模基准来评估和训练支持这种多层次"构思启发"的检索系统，因此需要构建此类资源以推动研究。

Method: RATIO通过一种通用配方构建：将此前仅用于分类任务的"话语标记远程监督"（discourse-marker distant supervision）扩展到语料库规模检索场景，从数百万篇CS领域全文论文中构建基准，并结合大语言模型（LLM）与人工审核进行质量把关。基准定义了三种构思操作：Address（检索解决特定问题的潜在方法）、Broaden（检索更泛化的问题表述）、Specify（检索具体的实例化方案）。

Result: 实验表明，针对特定操作进行微调能够显著提升检索器性能，但距离最优表现仍有较大提升空间，说明该任务仍有研究余地。

Conclusion: RATIO为文献驱动的科学构思提供了一套可扩展的训练与评估框架，开辟了"科学灵感检索"这一新的研究方向。

Abstract: Retrieved scientific literature can serve as inspiration for both human and AI scientists. Inspiration can take different forms: prior work may directly suggest how to address a problem, or surface directions at different levels of abstraction - zooming out to a more general view or zooming in to a concrete realization. We introduce RATIO (Retrieval Across Typed Ideation Operations), a large-scale benchmark in which relevance is defined by three operations which we name ideation moves: Address retrieves potential approaches for stated problems, Broaden retrieves more general formulations, and Specify retrieves concrete instantiations. RATIO is constructed from millions of full-text scientific papers across CS literature via a general recipe that extends discourse-marker distant supervision - previously used only for classification - to corpus-scale retrieval, combined with extensive LLM and human vetting. Experiments show that operation-specific fine-tuning substantially boosts retrievers but leaves much room for further improvements. RATIO provides a scalable training and evaluation framework for retrieval components that support literature-grounded ideation, opening up new research avenues on scientific inspiration retrieval.

</details>


### [206] [Making Clinical Language Models Auditable: Concept-Guided Fine-Tuning for Robust Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27397)
*Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CAST框架，利用稀疏自编码器暴露并抑制临床语言模型中的笔记特定伪影特征，在不损失性能的前提下实现可审计的临床文本分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型在院内数据上表现良好，但在部署环境变化时因利用笔记特定伪影（如模板、分隔符、套话）而性能骤降。这些伪影并不反映患者真实状态，却成为模型预测的隐式线索，导致模型缺乏鲁棒性和可审计性。

Method: CAST包含四个核心组件：1）利用稀疏自编码器从Transformer中间激活中提取稀疏、人类可审计的特征；2）通过LLM辅助解释管道和ICD-10检索约束为SAE潜在特征标注语义标签；3）在微调过程中通过残差减法抑制已识别的伪影潜在特征；4）提供事后逐概念归因以支持模型决策审计。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV出院记录死亡率预测任务上，CAST相比对应的微调编码器基线有显著提升，且与强LLM基线保持竞争力，同时生成了支持每个预测的临床概念特征级审计轨迹，以及训练中抑制的伪影概念清单。

Conclusion: CAST证明了通过显式暴露并抑制伪影特征而非依赖隐式正则化，可以在保持模型性能的同时显著提升部署鲁棒性和可解释性，为临床文本分类提供了可审计的解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical language models can achieve strong in-hospital accuracy yet fail under deployment shifts because they exploit note-specific artifacts (e.g., templates, separators, boilerplate) that do not reflect patient state. We propose CAST (Concept-guided Artifact Suppression Tuning), an SAE-based framework for auditable clinical text classification. CAST uses Sparse Autoencoders to expose sparse, human-auditable features from intermediate Transformer activations, labels SAE latents with an LLM-assisted interpretation pipeline and ICD-10 retrieval constraints, suppresses verified artifact latents via residual subtraction during fine-tuning, and provides post-hoc per-concept attributions for auditing model decisions. On MIMIC-IV discharge-note mortality prediction, CAST improves over its corresponding fine-tuned encoder baselines and remains competitive with strong LLM baselines, while producing a feature-level audit trail of the clinical concepts that support each prediction and the artifact concepts suppressed during training.

</details>


### [207] [Consolidating RLVR Capabilities Across Domains: A Deep Dive into Fusion Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27409)
*Siye Wu,Kai Yang,Yuchen Cai,Xin Xu,Peng-Yuan Wang,Jiaxuan Wang,Jiashun Liu,Jiafei Lyu,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种将独立领域专家大语言模型融合为统一模型的范式：Merge（合并任务向量）、Mix RL（混合数据集强化学习）和MOPD（多教师在线蒸馏）。实验发现三者平均性能差异不超过1.4分，但在单一基准上差距可达8.6分，且领域级差异与任务向量几何中的跨域关系一致。研究还揭示了各自的训练约束，并给出了实用选型指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）可以提升大语言模型的特定能力，但覆盖多种能力通常需要训练多个领域专家并随后进行整合。目前存在三种融合范式（Merge、Mix RL、MOPD），但它们在多数研究中被独立探讨，缺乏系统的横向比较，导致在实际场景中如何选择合适的方法尚不明确。

Method: 作者在共享专家和数据控制条件下，跨多个模型规模和多领域基准套件上对三种融合范式进行统一比较：Merge通过合并专家任务向量实现融合；Mix RL将各领域数据集混合进行强化学习；MOPD同时复用专家数据和专家作为教师进行在线蒸馏。研究考察了平均性能、单基准性能差距、域间差异与任务向量几何的关系，以及训练动态中的约束特性。

Result: 三种方法平均性能差异不超过1.4分，但单一基准上最大差距达8.6分；领域级性能变化与任务向量几何中体现的跨域关系相关。Mix RL受限于领域混合比例，MOPD受限于教师能力上限，Merge将所有专家更新压缩为一个。三者均能提升单样本准确率，且不损害样本覆盖率和保留能力。

Conclusion: 作者提出实用选型指南：已有专家且注重低成本融合时选Merge；无专家需训练统一模型时选Mix RL并调整域比例以利于跨域迁移；需要保留各领域特定增益且不追求超越教师或最小化端到端成本时选MOPD。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves specific capabilities of large language models, but covering multiple capabilities often involves training separate domain experts and subsequently consolidating them. We organize three fusion paradigms by the artefacts they reuse: Merge combines expert task vectors, Mix RL pools their datasets, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) uses both. Because they have largely been studied in isolation, how they compare and how to choose among them remain unclear. We compare all three using shared experts and data across model scales and a multi-domain benchmark suite. Although their average performance differs by at most 1.4 points, the gap reaches 8.6 points on a single benchmark, with domain-level variation tracking cross-domain relations visible in task-vector geometry. Training dynamics expose distinct constraints: Mix RL depends on domain mixture proportions, MOPD remains bounded by its teachers, and Merge compresses all expert updates into one. All three improve single-sample accuracy without measurable gains in solution coverage or losses in held-out capabilities. These results yield a practical guideline: use Merge when experts already exist and cheap fusion is paramount; Mix RL when training a unified model without experts, with domain proportions adjusted for cross-domain transfer; and MOPD when preserving domain-specific gains matters more than surpassing teachers or minimizing end-to-end cost.

</details>


### [208] [Boosting LLM Exploration via Weak-Model Guidance in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27420)
*Xingyu Shen,Huishuai Zhang,Peng Li,Yinchun Wang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种通过外部弱模型生成的部分推理轨迹作为前缀来保持RLVR训练中生成多样性的方法，有效缓解熵崩塌并扩展推理覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽显著提升LLM推理能力，但常导致策略熵下降，缩小推理覆盖范围，损害大规模pass@k性能。现有方法主要通过算法正则化缓解熵崩塌，但忽略了跨模型非参数扰动这一途径。

Method: 不使用仅依赖模型内部探索的方式，而是强制目标模型基于由更小、更弱的语言模型生成的部分推理轨迹来生成答案。这些不熟悉的前缀可有效打破过度自信，鼓励模型探索不同的推理路径。作者还实证研究了外部前缀的作用机制，分析了分布差异对RLVR训练中探索动力学的影响。

Result: 在多个数学基准上的实验表明，该方法持续优于vanilla RLVR。尤其随着k值增大，性能提升愈发显著，证明推理覆盖范围得到了实质性扩展。该方法无需额外的SFT、复杂的奖励设计或提示工程即可有效缓解熵崩塌。

Conclusion: 利用外部弱模型生成的部分推理轨迹作为前缀，是一种简单且有效的保持LLM在RLVR训练中生成多样性的方法，在不增加训练复杂度的前提下扩展推理覆盖并缓解熵崩塌。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) significantly improves LLM reasoning but often causes a drop in policy entropy, leading to narrowed reasoning coverage and degraded pass@$k$ for large $k$. While existing methods mitigate this entropy collapse through algorithmic regularizations, cross-model non-parametric perturbation is also neglected. In this work, we propose a simple yet effective approach to preserve the generative diversity of LLMs during RLVR. Instead of relying solely on internal exploration, we force the target model to generate answers based on partial reasoning trajectories generated by a smaller, weaker language models. These unfamiliar prefixes effectively disrupt over-confidence and encourage the exploration of distinct reasoning paths. We empirically study the potential of outer prefixes, revealing the mechanism of the impact of distributional discrepancy to the exploration dynamics in RLVR training. Experiments across multiple mathematical benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms vanilla RLVR. Notably, the performance gain becomes increasingly pronounced as $k$ scales up, demonstrating a substantial expansion of reasoning coverage. Furthermore, our approach efficiently mitigates entropy collapse without requiring additional SFT, intricate reward designs, or complex prompting.

</details>


### [209] [Stochastic Estimation of Transduced Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27428)
*Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson,Samuel Kiegeland,Manuel de Prada Corral,Ryan Cotterell,Tim Vieira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无放回重采样的束求和算法，用于估计转换语言模型（TLM）的目标前缀概率，解决了传统阈值剪枝束求和产生的有偏下限和未知误差问题。该算法通过无放回重采样提供无偏估计，并在文本和DNA任务上取得了更好的计算-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转换语言模型（TLM）将预训练源语言模型与有限状态转换器相结合，在计算目标前缀概率时需要求和所有映射到该前缀的源字符串的概率，该集合可能指数增长甚至无限大。现有方法采用基于源前缀概率的计算捷径并配合阈值剪枝束求和进行近似，但这只能得到误差未知的下限，缺乏无偏性保证。

Method: 本文提出无放回地重采样源前缀，并为每个选中的前缀按其包含概率的倒数进行重新加权。递归应用此校正即可得到目标前缀概率的无偏估计器，同时能够估计阈值剪枝所损失的概率质量。算法在扩展保留的源前缀的过程中，根据已添加的概率质量逐步采样决定要保留的前缀，从而缩减前缀数量，节省计算量并保证以概率1终止。

Result: 在百科全书文本和DNA任务上与顺序蒙特卡洛（SMC）基线比较：在文本上取得更好的计算-方差权衡，在DNA上以相同最大粒子数取得更低的误差。在DNA到氨基酸的转换任务中，相比阈值剪枝束求和将运行时间降低了数个数量级，使长目标字符串的前缀概率估计变得可行。在已发表的阅读时间分析中，用无偏采样替代阈值剪枝显著降低了语料库惊讶度估计值，但不改变已发表的结论。

Conclusion: 无放回重采样束求和算法为TLM前缀概率估计提供了无偏且高效的方法，在保持计算效率的同时克服了原有阈值剪枝方法的系统性偏差问题，广泛适用于文本和生物学序列等场景。

Abstract: Transduced language models (TLMs) compose a pretrained \emph{source} language model with a functional finite-state transducer to induce a language model over \emph{target} strings. Computing the probability of a target prefix under a TLM amounts to summing the source-model probabilities of all source strings that the transducer maps to target strings beginning with that prefix. This set can be exponentially large or infinite. Prior work uses a computational shortcut based on source prefix probabilities, then approximates the resulting sum with threshold-pruned beam summing. This produces a lower bound with unknown error. Instead, we resample source prefixes without replacement and reweight each selected prefix by the inverse of its inclusion probability. We show that applying this correction recursively gives an unbiased estimator of the target prefix probability and lets us estimate the mass lost by threshold pruning. Our beam-summing algorithm extends the retained source prefixes and samples which prefixes to keep, reducing their number as more probability mass is added to the running estimate. This can save computation and guarantees that the run halts with probability one. We evaluate the method on encyclopedic text and DNA against sequential Monte Carlo baselines that resample with replacement. It achieves a better compute--variance tradeoff on text and lower error at the same maximum number of particles on DNA. On a DNA-to-amino-acid transduction, it reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude relative to threshold-pruned beam summing and makes estimating prefix probabilities for long target strings feasible. Replacing threshold pruning with unbiased sampling in a published reading-time analysis substantially lowers the estimated corpus surprisal but leaves the published conclusions unchanged.

</details>


### [210] [TTPO: Test-Time Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27448)
*Aozhe Wang,Zhengxi Lu,Jianze Wang,Shangke Lv,Ying Liu,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Hua Yang,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO)，一种无需真实标签的测试时训练方法，通过不对称目标函数分别蒸馏同意的rollout和惩罚不同意的rollout，在竞赛级数学推理基准上匹配标签监督的OPSD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练方法（如RL和OPSD）依赖ground-truth标签，无法用于测试时训练（TTT）。虽然用多数投票伪标签替代真实标签是自然选择，但伪标签错误时会导致教师模型被污染并误导所有token。

Method: 观察到失败模式的不对称性：与伪标签不一致的rollout无论投票是否正确通常是错的。基于此提出TTPO，采用不对称目标：通过OPSD蒸馏一致的rollout，用Grouped RL惩罚不一致的rollout。token级选择进一步优化两个分支：蒸馏降低已收敛位置的权重，RL只惩罚高置信度错误。多数投票路由在模型改进时提供更紧的自监督信号。

Result: 无需任何标签，TTPO在五个竞赛级基准上匹配标签监督的OPSD；将Qwen3-1.7B的TTT准确率从38.0%提升到45.2%；无思考模式下提升+25.2%至+36.4%；展现出强跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: TTPO通过不对称地处理与伪标签一致和不一致的rollout，在不依赖真实标签的情况下实现了与标签监督方法相当的测试时训练性能，有效解决了伪标签错误导致教师模型被污染的问题。

Abstract: Recent prominent post-training methods, such as Reinforcement Learning (RL) and On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), have driven rapid progress in mathematical reasoning for large language models, yet their reliance on ground-truth labels precludes test-time training (TTT). Replacing ground truth with majority-vote pseudo-labels is a natural alternative, yet it is fragile: an incorrect vote corrupts the teacher and misleads every token. We observe that this failure mode is asymmetric: rollouts that disagree with the pseudo-label are typically wrong regardless of whether the vote itself is correct. Building on this observation, we propose Test-Time Policy Optimization (TTPO), an asymmetric objective that distills agreeing rollouts via OPSD and penalizes disagreeing rollouts with Grouped RL. Token-level selection further refines both branches: distillation down-weights already-converged positions, while RL penalizes only confident errors. Both updates remain well-grounded even under frequent pseudo-label errors, and majority-vote routing yields tighter self-supervision as the model improves. Without any labels, TTPO matches label-supervised OPSD on five competition-level benchmarks, raises Qwen3-1.7B from 38.0% to 45.2% in TTT, yields +25.2% to +36.4% without thinking, and shows strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [211] [CritICL: Inference-Time Weak-to-Strong Generalization from Small Language Model Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27455)
*Yufan Wu,Yinghui He,Zhengyi Hu,Lang Wei,Ruichen Li,Qifan Yang,Ting Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CritICL是一种新型推理时框架，利用同家族模型中较弱模型产生的结构化失败模式作为指导信号，通过基于批评的情境示例（critique-based in-context examples）来提升LLM推理能力，同时保持高效率和低token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法（inference-time scaling）虽然能显著提升LLM推理性能，但通常依赖重复生成或外部验证，导致计算开销大、效率低。作者希望找到一种无需大量采样即可提升推理能力的方法。

Method: 作者观察到同一模型家族内不同规模的LLM在失败模式上存在结构化规律。基于这一洞察，CritICL将弱模型的失败模式作为有价值的指导信号，融入到推理过程中的情境示例里。方法包含两个变体：CritICL-dynamic通过自适应预测输入特定的失败模式并检索对应批评；CritICL-static则使用全局失败模式档案提供稳定的指导。

Result: 实验表明CritICL一致优于标准情境学习（in-context learning），在性能上可与甚至超过测试时扩展方法（test-time scaling methods）相媲美，同时所需生成次数和token成本显著更低。

Conclusion: CritICL证明了一种高效的推理增强范式：将"失败"而非"成功"作为学习素材，利用跨模型规模的失败模式转移来提升推理性能，在效率与效果之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Recent advances in inference-time scaling have significantly improved the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs). However, these methods typically rely on repeated generation or external verification. To address this limitation, we introduce CritICL, a novel inference-time framework that improves reasoning while maintaining high efficiency. Our key insight is that LLM failure modes exhibit structured patterns across model scales within the same family. Instead of treating failures as undesirable outputs, CritICL leverages them as a source of guidance. Specifically, we utilize failure modes derived from weaker models and incorporate them into inference through critique-based in-context examples. We propose two variants: CritICL-dynamic, which adaptively predicts input-specific failure modes and retrieves critiques, and CritICL-static, which uses a global failure mode profile to provide stable guidance. Experimental results show that CritICL consistently outperforms standard in-context learning and achieves performance competitive with or superior to test-time scaling methods, while requiring significantly fewer generations and lower token cost. Code available at: https://github.com/umwyf/CRITICL

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SLM条件层级关系路由（SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing）架构，将小型语言模型集成到图消息选择中，通过目标条件化路由查询实现邻域消息和关系层级的信息选择与聚合，在保留拓扑结构证据的同时引入语义信息辅助预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络将带标签属性图（Labeled Property Graphs）中的异质文本和类别属性表示为静态特征向量，无法根据特定预测目标动态决定哪些语义证据应影响消息传播过程，从而限制了语义信息在图学习中的作用。

Method: 架构由两部分组成：①拓扑GNN提供稳定的结构表示和预测锚点；②参数高效的小型语言模型处理结构化图软令牌（structured graph soft tokens），生成目标条件化路由查询。该查询先在每个关系类型内选择相关消息，再跨关系级摘要进行信息路由，最终产生有界残差更新（bounded residual update）作用于拓扑锚点。

Result: 架构在邻域和关系类型两个层面支持可解释性分析，为属性丰富的图学习提供了将语言派生语义集成到消息传递中的通用机制，并保证语义信息对预测进行有界修改而不破坏结构证据。

Conclusion: 通过将SLM直接嵌入图消息选择过程而非仅作为静态特征编码器，实现了目标自适应的语义感知消息路由，为带标签属性图上的预测任务提供了一种兼顾结构稳定性和语义灵活性的新范式。

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [213] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuronFuzz是一个白盒模糊测试框架，通过利用LLM内部安全神经元的激活作为连续执行反馈来评估模型安全性，消除了生成响应作为反馈的需求，在21个文本和多模态模型上实现了76-100%的越狱发现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化安全测试方法依赖响应级反馈，每个候选提示都需要生成目标模型的响应来评估攻击效果，这不仅成本高昂，而且对于强对齐模型只能提供稀疏的引导（大多数候选被拒绝且得到相同失败结果），导致测试效率低下。

Method: NeuronFuzz通过以下组件实现：(1) SafetyOracle：将安全神经元激活转化为连续的越狱警报分数，在prefill阶段即可获得，无需响应生成；(2) 使用模板不变的有害/良性输入和稳定性感知选择来识别紧凑的安全神经元集合；(3) 利用警报分数的可微性，通过梯度识别安全敏感模板位置，并使用掩码语言模型生成流畅、上下文兼容的变异，同时保留有害载荷。

Result: 在5个白盒源模型上达到76-100%的越狱发现率，比基线高出最多48个百分点；优化后的模板可零样本迁移到开源权重和6个专有目标模型，平均ASR/Top-5集成ASR分别为69.6%/92.6%和44.1%/60.0%。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz证明利用安全神经元激活作为连续的内部反馈可以显著提高LLM安全评估的效率和效果，同时生成的攻击模板具有良好的跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [214] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于PyTorch的研究型框架，集成了冻结和剪枝机制用于设计和优化二值化神经网络（BNN）。在该框架基础上，作者提出了一种考虑跨抽象层级参数相对重要性的全局加权剪枝方法，在VGG11上实现了70%的剪枝率且精度保持不变，而现有最先进方法在二值化设定下仅达到41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的极端压缩（如完全二值化）能显著减少内存占用和计算复杂度，便于在FPGA和微控制器等受限边缘硬件上部署。虽然将二值化与剪枝结合有望带来额外效率提升，但现有剪枝策略不适用于二值化表示，且很少能转化为实际的硬件资源节省。

Method: 提出一个基于PyTorch的研究导向框架，包含冻结（freezing）和剪枝（pruning）机制，用于设计优化二值化神经网络。框架支持快速可复现地评估现有先进方法以及快速原型开发新方法。在此基础上提出一种考虑学习参数跨抽象层级相对重要性的新颖剪枝方法，采用全局加权机制。

Result: 全局加权机制在模型精度与剪枝率之间始终取得更优的权衡，在VGG11上实现了70%的剪枝率且精度保持不变，而现有最先进方法在二值化设定下仅达到41%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架和剪枝方法有效解决了二值化神经网络中剪枝策略不适配的问题，通过跨抽象层级的全局重要性加权，实现了显著优于现有方法的剪枝性能，为二值化网络在受限硬件上的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [215] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面证明Muon优化器中有限深度的Newton-Schulz迭代不仅不是近似误差，反而在非光滑非凸优化中具有有益效果：它通过平滑不连续的极分解映射，使Muon能够收敛到驻点，而对数深度的Newton-Schulz迭代就足以保证收敛，且样本复杂度达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大模型预训练中表现出色，但现有理论要么用精确极分解替代Newton-Schulz迭代，要么将其视为近似误差，导致Muon实际运行的迭代反而被理论认为有损。这产生了理论与实践的矛盾：既然实践效果好，理论上应能解释其优势而非仅视为缺陷。

Method: 通过在线学习到非凸优化的转化框架（online-to-nonconvex conversion），将Muon的更新规则视为在线学习器，利用其遗憾界推导驻点收敛保证。关键洞察是：有限深度的Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极分解映射平滑化为关于奇异值的Lipschitz映射，从而Muon可被视为带有平滑谱势的在线学习器。

Result: 证明Newton-Schulz深度仅需随目标精度对数增长即可保证非光滑非凸优化收敛到驻点，而精确极分解更新可能不收敛。样本复杂度匹配非光滑非凸优化的最佳已知界，且在光滑非凸优化中达到问题相关因子意义下的最优。论证可推广到具有同样平滑性质的任意谱映射。

Conclusion: 有限深度Newton-Schulz迭代在Muon中的角色应重新理解：它不是对精确极分解的劣质近似，而是通过平滑化赋予Muon理论上的收敛保证。这一结论弥合了理论与实践之间的鸿沟，并为设计基于谱映射的优化器提供了新视角。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [216] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMELS框架，通过快速构建邻域图和代数多重网格求解器加速标签传播（Label Spreading）半监督学习，显著提升大规模高维数据集上的运行效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标签传播方法在大规模高维数据集上受限于计算成本和内存约束，且现有实现难以高效扩展，需要一种更快速且对超参数更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: AMELS包含两个关键组件：一是快速构建邻域图以加速图构造阶段；二是引入代数多重网格（AMG）求解器替代传统的随机游走迭代，利用多层级特性在单个多重网格周期内完成任意规模图上的标签传播。

Result: 实验表明AMELS相较现有实现显著减少运行时间，同时对超参数选择在运行时间和分类精度两方面均更鲁棒，在仅有少量标注样本的大规模图像数据集上仍能产生准确标签。

Conclusion: AMELS使标签传播能够高效应用于大规模图像数据集，在极少量标注样本条件下仍保持高分类精度，为半监督学习在大规模场景下的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [217] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出针对推理时对齐策略Best-of-N (BoN)的隐私保护方法。通过在奖励分数中加入校准的Gumbel噪声，同时解决奖励黑客和隐私泄露两个问题。进一步提出PrivITP方法，将χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制结合，实现与响应数量无关的隐私成本。实验证明两种方法均优于存在关键数量限制的BoN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Best-of-N采样是最广泛使用的推理时对齐策略，但其面临两个核心问题：一是奖励黑客（reward hacking），即所选响应会利用代理奖励模型的误差；二是用于训练奖励模型的敏感人类偏好数据缺乏隐私保护。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题，且BoN在采样数量超过临界值后性能会退化。

Method: 1) PrivBoN：在奖励分数选择前加入校准尺度的Gumbel噪声，同时实现ε-差分隐私和KL正则化对齐。当隐私预算超过临界阈值ε*时，隐私所需的噪声即为遗憾最优的正则化项。2) PrivITP：结合χ²正则化拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制，实现ex-post (ε,δ)-DP，其隐私成本与响应数量n无关，将正则化参数与隐私参数解耦。

Result: 实验涵盖多个语言模型、数据集和奖励模型，验证了：PrivBoN和PrivITP具有缩放单调性（而BoN在临界n之后退化）；在相同隐私水平下PrivITP匹配或优于PrivBoN；在强隐私约束下PrivITP优势最大。

Conclusion: 单一干预（奖励分数加入校准噪声）即可同时解决奖励黑客和隐私保护两个问题。利用Gumbel噪声的分布特性，可以将差分隐私所需的噪声自然转化为KL正则化，实现零额外对齐代价。PrivITP进一步解耦了隐私与正则化参数，在强隐私场景下更具优势。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OpEmbed框架，利用大规模生产环境中的隐私保护支持案例元数据（不含文本内容）来学习LLM云服务的紧凑操作指纹表示，在Google Cloud的33,000多个生产案例（覆盖7个LLM家族、26个月）上验证了其在模型选型、操作预测和故障迁移等方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前托管LLM服务已成为生产系统的一部分，但模型选择和服务规划仍主要依赖能力基准测试，这些基准对部署后的操作行为揭示甚少。现有评估范式无法反映LLM服务在实际生产环境中的运营表现，亟需一种能捕捉LLM云服务操作特征的框架。

Method: OpEmbed将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道操作签名，通过时间对比学习（temporal contrastive learning）、跨视图重建（cross-view reconstruction）和生成序正则化（generational-ordinality regularization）来学习低维表示。整个框架仅利用结构化的、隐私保护的（不含案例文本）支持案例元数据。

Result: 在Google Cloud的超过33,000个生产支持案例（横跨7个LLM家族、26个月）上，OpEmbed恢复了可解释的家族级和版本级结构；在留一模型（leave-one-model-out）操作预测中优于非学习基线；在有限的早期窗口数据下依然有效；并支持跨模型故障类型迁移。

Conclusion: OpEmbed为LLM云服务的操作行为建模提供了实用且可扩展的框架，可用于模型上架、支持就绪度评估和操作监控等场景，并总结了构建与评估该工具的实际经验教训。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [219] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CG4AI框架，通过主线性规划与定价子问题构建AI模型的凸组合，在输出上强制线性约束，保证模型满足预定义规则，并在MNIST和SNDLIB基准上实现了比单模型基线更好的精度与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习仅最小化损失函数，无法保证模型输出满足预定义规则或约束。在许多真实应用（如自主系统和网络路由）中，这种约束满足至关重要，因此需要一种能够在训练过程中显式引入并强制输出约束的方法。

Method: CG4AI框架采用列生成（column generation）思想：主问题是一个线性规划（LP），用于确定多个AI模型的凸组合权重；定价子问题利用LP对偶变量生成新模型，聚焦于最违反约束的方向；切割平面过程将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。

Result: 在MNIST上展示了四种约束应用场景：纯约束学习、提升对抗鲁棒性、修正误分类样本、强制输出重标记。在SNDLIB标准网络上的多商品流问题中，CG4AI可靠地产生可行预测器，且精度优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI是一种通用的约束感知模型组合框架，能够在保证输出可行性的同时提升预测精度，适用于分类任务和网络路由等结构化输出问题。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [220] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"潜在诊断分类法"（Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy），一种为分类器构建安全防护层并开发互补诊断的方法框架，通过维度优化的分类器、潜在支持向量识别和攻击标记构建诊断分类，将提示分为可安全依赖、启发式偏差/覆盖、以及需人工审查的三类，并在提示注入数据集上验证了约77%的高置信决策对单token移除不鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类器在高置信度下做出的决策并不总是可靠，需要一种系统性的方法来识别哪些自信决策可以信任，哪些存在脆弱性或可利用的捷径，从而为分类器部署提供安全防护层。

Method: 框架包含三步：(i) 构建维度优化的分类器，嵌入维度通过交叉验证性能而非先验固定选取；(ii) 定位较小规模的潜在支持向量集（约29%训练样本）代表有影响力的提示，用于识别改变预测标签的token；(iii) 利用这些token及其攻击强度构建诊断分类法，将提示分类为可安全依赖、启发式偏差/覆盖、以及上下文不足需人工审查三类。

Result: 在公开的提示注入数据集上，约77%的高置信决策对移除单个token不鲁棒，此脆弱性分为两类失效模式：置信度校准失败和真正可利用的捷径。针对分类法每个区域还推荐了修复策略。

Conclusion: 该框架以一系列步骤展示并验证了其操作流程，为分类器部署提供端到端的诊断和修复指南，帮助区分需要不同处理策略的提示，增强了分类器的安全性和可解释性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [221] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedCMAPSS，一个基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集的联邦学习剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计基准，定义了五个标准化任务（从理想的IID到极端统计异构）来模拟真实的工业场景，并系统评估了多种联邦优化算法和神经网络架构，为联邦预测性维护提供了可复现的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于数据的预测性健康管理是工业4.0的关键使能技术，但RUL估计模型的开发常受限于run-to-failure数据的稀缺。虽然联邦学习可以在不共享传感器数据的情况下协作训练模型，但现有研究缺乏统一的评估框架，导致不同方法之间难以进行公平比较。

Method: 构建FedCMAPSS基准，基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集定义五个标准化任务，覆盖从理想IID设置到极端统计异构性的多种现实工业场景；系统评估多种最先进的联邦优化算法在多个神经网络架构上的表现，并公开源代码和数据划分以支持可复现性。

Result: 建立了可复现的联邦RUL估计基线，系统比较了不同联邦优化算法和神经网络架构在五种异构性任务下的性能，并公开了源代码和数据划分。

Conclusion: FedCMAPSS为联邦预测性维护解决方案的开发和比较提供了标准基础，填补了该领域缺乏统一评估框架的空白。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [222] [NeoTriFuse: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Fusion under Missingness Heterogeneity for Neonatal Mortality Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26436)
*Jiyuan Tian,Qincheng Shen,Ye Lin,Yu Gao,Haohui Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeoTriFuse，一种可靠性感知的多模态融合框架，用于处理新生儿重症监护中缺失率不均的床旁监测数据。通过将缺失视为显式可靠性信号来动态调节模态贡献，集成围产期静态变量、局部-全局时序编码器及患者级统计摘要，联合优化死亡预测与住院时长辅助目标，在F1 0.6736和AUROC 0.9454上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿死亡率预测面临极端类别不平衡、异质临床风险因素、多尺度时间动态和大量数据缺失等挑战。传统方法将缺失仅视为预处理问题，忽略了缺失模式本身携带的临床信息，需要一种能显式建模缺失并动态调节模态贡献的新框架。

Method: NeoTriFuse采用三支路结构：(1) 静态围产期变量特征；(2) 局部-全局时序编码器捕获多尺度时间动态；(3) 患者级统计摘要。通过可靠性引导的门控机制将缺失率作为可靠性信号，动态调整各模态在融合中的权重。此外，联合优化死亡预测主目标与住院时长辅助目标以增强表示学习。

Result: F1分数为0.6736±0.0216，AUROC为0.9454±0.0056。消融实验显示局部-全局时序架构和患者级统计摘要分支贡献最大，可靠性感知门控在阈值依赖指标上提供额外提升。敏感性分析表明在邻近超参数设置下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的多模态融合是处理真实临床缺失条件下新生儿死亡预测的实用方法，支持将缺失建模为信息的而非简单的数据质量问题。

Abstract: Neonatal mortality risk prediction from bedside monitoring data remains challenging due to extreme class imbalance, heterogeneous clinical risk factors, multi-scale temporal dynamics, and substantial missingness. We propose NeoTriFuse, a reliability-aware multimodal fusion framework for missingness-heterogeneous neonatal monitoring data. Unlike conventional multimodal approaches that treat missingness primarily as a preprocessing issue, NeoTriFuse models missingness as an explicit reliability signal that dynamically modulates modality contributions during fusion. The framework integrates static perinatal variables, local-global temporal encoders, and patient-level statistical summaries through reliability-guided gating mechanisms, while jointly optimizing mortality prediction and an auxiliary length-of-stay objective. NeoTriFuse achieves competitive performance, with an F1 score of 0.6736 +/- 0.0216 and an AUROC of 0.9454 +/- 0.0056. Ablation studies indicate that the local-global temporal architecture and patient-level summary branch contribute most substantially to predictive performance, while reliability-aware gating provides additional improvements on threshold-dependent metrics under heterogeneous observation completeness. Sensitivity analyses further suggest stable performance across nearby hyperparameter settings. Overall, the findings support reliability-aware multimodal fusion as a practical approach for neonatal mortality prediction under realistic clinical missingness conditions.

</details>


### [223] [Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26437)
*Haohui Lu,jiyuan Tian,Fangyu Zhou,Shahadat Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SF-GNN，一个轻量级、架构无关的框架，通过在消息传递步骤中执行随机边过滤来抑制敏感同质性和结构传播放大器（枢纽参与、三角闭包）驱动的偏差传播，并结合带预热调度的统计奇偶性正则化器，在五个基准数据集上实现公平性与准确性的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平感知GNN方法主要从全局层面约束表示或预测分布，没有显式控制图拓扑中偏差信息在聚合阶段传播的局部结构路径，导致在敏感同质性下即使排除敏感属性，不公平行为仍然存在。

Method: SF-GNN首先通过结合敏感同质性与结构传播放大器（枢纽参与、三角闭包）识别易偏边，然后在每个消息传递步骤中引入随机边过滤，选择性降低权重或移除这些边，同时保留其余图结构；训练中还引入带预热调度的统计奇偶性正则化器以稳定优化。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，SF-GNN在保持竞争力预测性能的同时实现一致的公平性改进，相较于近期公平感知GNN基线获得了更好的公平性-准确性权衡。

Conclusion: 通过从源头过滤结构偏差路径，SF-GNN提供了一种轻量且与架构无关的公平性增强方案，有效平衡了公平性与预测性能之间的冲突。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can exhibit unfair behavior even when sensitive attributes are excluded from node features, because graph topology and message passing propagate group-correlated signals under sensitive homophily. Existing fairness-aware GNN methods mainly constrain representations or prediction distributions at a global level, without explicitly controlling the local structural pathways through which biased information propagates during aggregation. We propose Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks (SF-GNN), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that mitigates structural bias at its source. SF-GNN identifies bias-prone edges by combining sensitive homophily with structural propagation amplifiers, including hub participation and triadic closure. It then incorporates stochastic edge filtering into each message-passing step to selectively downweight or remove these edges while preserving the remaining graph structure. Training further incorporates a statistical-parity regularizer with a warm-up schedule to stabilize optimization. Experiments on five benchmark datasets show that SF-GNN achieves consistent fairness improvements while maintaining competitive predictive performance, leading to a better fairness--accuracy trade-off than recent fairness-aware GNN baselines.

</details>


### [224] [Toward Equitable Low-Carbon Mobility: Fairness-Aware Demand Prediction for Expanding Bike-Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26451)
*Man Luo,Yixuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairGIN是一个面向扩张中共享单车系统的公平性感知图神经网络，通过扩展模拟增量训练、注意力知识迁移和公平性优化三个组件，在保持预测精度的同时减少收入层级间的不平等资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统持续扩张带来两个核心挑战：(1) 新部署站点缺乏历史骑行记录，导致图神经网络在动态网络上面临冷启动训练的分布不匹配问题；(2) 历史需求数据可能编码结构性不平等——低收入社区的较低骑行量反映的是基础设施接入受限而非潜在需求薄弱，直接基于此类数据训练会强化现有的出行不平等。

Method: FairGIN包含三个核心组件：(1) 扩张模拟增量训练（Expansion-Simulated Increment Training）在训练过程中随机模拟网络扩张，缩小冷启动分布差距；(2) 注意力知识迁移（Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer）结合站点自适应温度缩放与正交嵌入对齐，将数据丰富的既有站点表示迁移到数据稀疏的新站点；(3) 公平性感知优化（Fairness-Aware Optimization）引入收入分层正则化和公平校准部署评分，促进更具包容性的站点布局。

Result: 在纽约市和西雅图数据集上的实验表明，FairGIN在多种扩张场景下均取得了最先进的预测精度，同时在不牺牲系统整体效率的前提下显著降低了基于收入的不平等。

Conclusion: FairGIN证明了在共享单车需求预测中，公平性与预测准确性可以同时兼顾，为扩张中的智能出行系统提供了兼顾公平与效率的建模范式。

Abstract: Bike-sharing systems are an important component of low-carbon urban mobility, but continued expansion creates challenges in both cold-start prediction and equitable resource allocation. Newly deployed stations lack historical ridership records, causing a mismatch between training and inference for graph-based models on evolving networks. Historical demand may also encode structural inequalities, as lower ridership in low-income neighborhoods can reflect limited infrastructure access rather than weak latent demand. Models trained directly on such data may therefore reinforce existing mobility disparities. We propose FairGIN, a fairness-aware graph neural network for demand prediction in expanding bike-sharing systems. FairGIN integrates three components. Expansion-Simulated Increment Training stochastically simulates network expansion during training to reduce the cold-start distribution gap. Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer combines station-adaptive temperature scaling with orthogonal embedding alignment to transfer representations from data-rich existing stations to data-sparse new stations. Fairness-Aware Optimization introduces income-stratified regularization and an equity-calibrated deployment score to support more inclusive station placement. Experiments on NYC and Seattle demonstrate that FairGIN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy across diverse expansion scenarios while substantially reducing income-based disparities without compromising overall system efficiency.

</details>


### [225] [Distributed Training using an Intelligent Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26453)
*Nihar Shah,Ben Blier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将广域网(WAN)作为分布式训练的主动参与者，通过多播技术和在线FPGA分别缓解出/入流量瓶颈，并设计优化框架生成绕网络拓扑的富同步调度（旋转团），在九城市拓扑上验证可缩小与同地训练之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨广域网做分布式训练极具挑战，因为有限带宽、高延迟和不均匀拓扑限制了计算孤岛之间的持续参数交换，现有方法难以充分利用WAN的能力。

Method: 系统层面：利用多播技术复制出站流量、利用在线FPGA聚合入站流量以解决出入口瓶颈。算法层面：构建优化框架，基于底层网络拓扑生成富同步调度（即旋转计算孤岛的团），最大化信息交换。

Result: 在基于DoubleZero网络建模的九城市拓扑（该网络为具备上述两种技术的可编程WAN）上展示了最优调度如何随网络能力而改变，验证了该方法可缩小与同地训练黄金标准之间的差距。

Conclusion: 将网络从被动的传输中介转化为训练的主动参与者，结合多播、在线FPGA与优化调度，可显著提升WAN场景下分布式训练的效率。

Abstract: Distributed training across a wide area network (WAN) is challenging, as continuous parameter exchange by islands of compute is constrained by limited bandwidth, high latency, and uneven topology. We propose making the network an active participant in training. On the systems side, such networks should leverage (i) multicast technology to replicate outbound traffic and (ii) in-line FPGAs to aggregate inbound traffic, to ease egress and ingress bottlenecks. These technologies are used for training across workers within a data center, but this paper extends them to the WAN. On the algorithms side, we develop an optimization framework that produces rich synchronization schedules (namely, rotating cliques of islands) around the underlying network topology and these technologies, to maximize information exchange. Finally, we illustrate this on a nine-city topology modeled on the DoubleZero network, a live programmable WAN equipped with both technologies, and show how the optimal schedules shift with the network's capabilities. Together, these can narrow the gap to the gold standard of colocated training.

</details>


### [226] [Diff Mining: Logit Differences Reveal Finetuning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26462)
*Greg Kocher,Robert West,Clément Dumas,Julian Minder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff Mining框架，通过对比微调模型与基础模型的logits差异来识别微调过程中模型学到了哪些行为/目标，仅需访问输出logits即可扩展到大型模型，无需模型内部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为语言模型中优化现有行为和引入新行为的黄金标准，但微调过程中究竟涌现了哪些行为往往不清晰。随着模型能力增强，理解微调内容变得日益重要，因为微调期间可能出现不良行为。现有模型diffing方法通常需要访问模型内部结构，限制了其可扩展性。

Method: Diff Mining由两个模块化阶段组成：(i) 在参考语料上提取微调模型与基础模型之间逐上下文的logits差异；(ii) 聚合所得信号以构建可解释的token集合来表征微调内容。聚合阶段探索了两种方法：简单Top-K频率方法和基于非负矩阵分解（NMF）的方法，后者可将多个微调目标解耦为不同的token簇。

Result: 实验表明Diff Mining在多个场景中表现优异：在微调领域检测任务中，显著优于最先进的模型diffing方法，无论是在识别相关token方面还是在下游可解释性代理任务中；在注入偏见的模型中，无需定向探测即可识别出超过三分之一的偏见。

Conclusion: Diff Mining展示了在开发审计工具以检测微调目标方面的潜力，为理解微调行为提供了一种简单、有效且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Finetuning has become the gold standard for refining existing behaviors and inducing new ones in language models, yet it often remains unclear exactly which behaviors emerge during this process. As models grow ever more capable, understanding finetuning better becomes increasingly important, particularly since unwanted behaviors may arise during finetuning. In this paper, we introduce Diff Mining, a simple yet effective framework for identifying what a finetuned model has learned by comparing its logits to those of its base model. Diff Mining effectively surfaces salient tokens that are amplified in the finetuned model, serving as a fingerprint of its training -- even on text unrelated to the finetuning domain. Unlike many existing model diffing methods which require model internals, Diff Mining only needs access to output logits and scales to large models. The framework consists of two modular stages: (i) extracting per-context logit differences between the finetuned and base models on a reference corpus, and (ii) aggregating the resulting signals to construct an interpretable token set representing the finetune. For aggregation, we explore both a simple Top-K frequency method and a Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF)-based approach for disentangling multiple finetuning objectives into distinct token clusters. Empirically, Diff Mining succeeds across diverse settings: on finetune domain detection, it significantly outperforms state-of-the-art model diffing methods both in identifying relevant tokens and in downstream performance when an interpretability agent is given access to the extracted token set; on models with injected biases, it identifies more than one third of the biases without targeted probing. Overall, our framework shows promise in developing auditing tools to detect finetuning objectives.

</details>


### [227] [Active Curriculum Refinement for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26469)
*Zhenya Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATH是一个基于课程图（DAG）的强化学习课程学习框架，通过主动学习采样多样化的课程路径并在未掌握区域重新分配训练样本，显式利用环境间的先修关系来提升训练的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中，许多环境之间存在先修关系（如难度递增或参数增量），这些关系构成有向无环课程图（DAG）。虽然这种结构经常被隐式利用，但显式建模它可以显著改善训练效果。

Method: PATH框架包含两个关键步骤：首先通过采样多样的课程路径来扩展覆盖范围，然后将训练资源重新分配到尚未掌握的区域。这两个步骤共同实现对课程图的主动学习。

Result: 在多种环境中的实验表明，PATH通过显式利用课程图结构，实现了强的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Conclusion: 显式利用课程图中的先修关系进行主动学习，可以显著提升强化学习的训练效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: In many reinforcement learning (RL) domains, environments are connected by prerequisite relations, such as difficulty-increasing edits or parameter increments, which induce a directed acyclic curriculum graph (DAG). Although this structure is often exploited only implicitly, explicitly modeling it can improve training. We introduce PATH, a curriculum-learning framework that performs active learning over the curriculum graph. PATH first expands coverage by sampling diverse curriculum paths and then reallocates training toward regions that remain unmastered. Experiments across diverse environments show that PATH explicitly leverages the graph structure to achieve strong robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [228] [Shared Actors Need Not Share Critics: Effects of Value Mismatch in Parallel Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26481)
*Zhenya Liu,Yang Meng,Zhuokai Zhao,Xuefeng Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究并行强化学习（多环境共享一个critic）中因不同环境对同一可见输入赋予不同期望回报而导致的"价值不匹配"（value mismatch）问题。作者通过bandit模型在理论上刻画了该问题如何偏置策略更新，并提出一个极简干预方案——仅向critic提供一个环境索引（多head条件critic），使其能够分离不同环境的价值目标。实验在CartPole、MuJoCo、BipedalWalker和Procgen等多种场景中验证了该方法，在16个Procgen游戏中平均提升40.8%的聚合归一化回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在并行强化学习中（如程序化生成关卡、随机动力学或课程学习），训练单个策略时通常所有环境共享一个critic。然而不同环境对critic可见的同一输入可能分配不同的期望回报，无环境信息的critic被迫在不同价值目标间折中，系统性地偏移各环境内的优势估计。这种价值不匹配会扭曲采样到的策略更新，强化无益动作、削弱甚至反转有用动作，这是传统的标量估计器方差无法捕捉的机制，现有研究缺乏对此问题的理论刻画与有效干预方案。

Method: 作者首先使用带多个环境和一个公共最优臂的bandit模型进行理论分析，证明使用无基线（oracle）、共享价值或环境特定价值作为baseline时，在固定策略下具有相同的平均logit更新且收敛到相同的最优策略，但实际学习路径可能差异显著。据此提出一个最小干预方案：不改变策略网络结构，仅向critic提供一个记录的环境索引（即多head条件critic），使critic能为不同环境分离价值目标。实验设计覆盖受控的CartPole和MuJoCo（验证预测的偏移值、优势与性能差距），以及更复杂的BipedalWalker和Procgen（验证学习稳定性与回报提升）。

Result: 受控实验（CartPole和MuJoCo）证实了理论预测的偏移价值、优势偏差和性能差距。在BipedalWalker和Procgen等更复杂场景中，多head条件critic干预带来更稳定的学习过程和更高的回报。在全部16个Procgen游戏中，该方法在每游戏600个未见关卡上的聚合归一化回报提升了40.8%。

Conclusion: 理论分析揭示：critic共享通过价值不匹配直接损害随机学习动态，这一机制无法仅由标量估计器的方差解释。价值不匹配会系统性重分配策略更新——强化无益动作、削弱有用动作。实验证明仅对环境索引进行条件化（multihead conditional critic）即可在并行强化学习中广泛有效，提供了一种极简、通用且显著的性能提升方案。

Abstract: When a single policy is trained in parallel across multiple environments of the same task, such as procedurally generated levels, randomized dynamics, or curricula, implementations commonly use one critic across all sampled environments. Yet different environments can assign different expected returns to the same input visible to the critic. A critic without environment information must then reconcile distinct value targets, systematically shifting the sampled advantages within individual environments. Using illustrative bandit models with multiple environments and a common optimal arm, we characterize how this value mismatch redistributes sampled policy updates, reinforcing unhelpful actions while attenuating or even reversing useful ones. The oracle processes using no baseline, the shared value, or the value specific to the sampled environment have the same mean logit update at a fixed policy and converge to the same optimal policy, yet their realized learning paths can differ sharply. The analysis motivates a minimal intervention: give only a logged environment index to the critic so that it can separate the value targets. Controlled CartPole and MuJoCo experiments expose the predicted shifted values, advantages, and performance gaps. In the more complex BipedalWalker and Procgen settings, the same intervention yields more stable learning and higher returns. Across all $16$ Procgen games, the multihead conditional critic improves aggregate normalized return on $600$ unseen levels per game by $40.8\%$. In conclusion, the theory identifies value mismatch as a direct mechanism through which critic sharing can degrade stochastic learning dynamics, not captured by scalar estimator variance alone, and the experiments show that conditioning on an index is broadly effective in parallel reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [229] [Bayesian methods and Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms for curve reconstruction and point cloud data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26490)
*Asir Intesar Tushar,Ioannis Sgouralis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种全贝叶斯框架，用于点云数据建模和闭合曲线重建，将观测点视为潜在位置的噪声扰动，并通过非参数先验正则化底层曲线，使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样器进行后验推断，在合成数据和真实LiDAR数据集上验证了重建精度和不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点云重建管线通常仅返回单一最优结构，缺乏不确定性量化；同时点云分析受大数据量、定位噪声和信息缺失的制约。需要一个能够同时实现准确重建并量化不确定性的贝叶斯框架。

Method: 构建全贝叶斯框架：观测点被建模为潜在位置的噪声扰动，潜在位置约束在底层曲线上，曲线通过非参数先验正则化；后验推断采用一系列针对点云特性定制的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器。

Result: 在合成数据和真实LiDAR数据集上的数值实验表明，该方法能够实现准确的重建，并对恢复的曲线提供量化的不确定性。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架为点云闭合曲线重建提供了同时兼顾准确性与不确定性量化的解决方案，可有效应对点云数据中的噪声和缺失问题。

Abstract: Point-cloud data routinely captured by modern imaging and sensor technologies provide detailed geometric descriptions of objects and environments, but their analysis is hindered by large data volumes, localization noise, and missing information. In addition, existing point-cloud reconstruction pipelines typically return a single best-fit structure without uncertainty quantification. We introduce a fully Bayesian framework for representing point-cloud data and reconstructing closed curves, in which observed points are modeled as noisy perturbations of latent locations constrained to lie on the underlying curve that is regularized by a non-parametric prior. Posterior inference in our framework is carried out using a series of Markov chain Monte Carlo samplers tailored to point-cloud characteristics. Numerical experiments, including synthetic examples and real-world LiDAR datasets, show accurate reconstructions and quantified uncertainty over the recovered curves.

</details>


### [230] [A Unified Framework for Fair and Personalized Decentralized Learning under Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26493)
*Krishnendu S. Tharakan,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DMFL-SQ算法，在去中心化学习中同时实现个性化、公平性和通信效率。该算法将基于图结构的个性化多任务学习与不可知混合公平性目标结合，并通过稀疏化、量化和事件触发同步压缩通信，在非凸目标下达到O(T^{-1/2})收敛速率，同时提供PAC-Bayes泛化保证，实验验证了其在降低通信开销的同时保持性能并提升客户端公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习系统在提升可扩展性、隐私和鲁棒性的同时，加剧了统计异质性、客户端公平性和通信约束三大挑战。现有研究往往分别处理这些问题，缺乏统一框架来回答"有限通信下去中心化学习能有多公平"这一核心问题。

Method: 提出DMFL-SQ算法：1) 在通信图上耦合个性化模型训练；2) 引入不可知混合公平性目标；3) 通过稀疏化、量化和事件触发同步三种机制压缩通信。理论方面，建立非凸目标的收敛保证，并推导公平感知混合目标的PAC-Bayes泛化界。

Result: DMFL-SQ在期望平方Moreau包络平稳性指标下达到O(T^{-1/2})收敛速率，与无压缩通信的方法同阶。在CIFAR-10和真实异质MUSMET EEG数据集上，DMFL-SQ显著降低通信量，同时保持预测性能并提升跨客户端公平性。

Conclusion: 理论分析与实验结果表明，去中心化学习中可以同时实现个性化、公平性和通信效率，且不牺牲主导收敛速率，证明这三者并非相互排斥的目标。

Abstract: Decentralized learning systems aim to collaboratively train models across multiple clients without relying on a central coordinator. While decentralization improves scalability, privacy, and robustness, it also exacerbates three fundamental challenges: statistical heterogeneity across clients, fairness in client-level performance, and stringent communication constraints. This raises a natural question: \emph{how fair can decentralized learning be under limited communication?} We address this question by presenting a unified framework for decentralized learning under communication constraints, bringing together graph-based personalization, agnostic fairness, and compressed event-triggered communication. Specifically, we propose a new algorithm DMFL-SQ, a decentralized multi-task learning algorithm that couples personalized model training over a communication graph with an agnostic mixture fairness objective, while reducing communication through sparsification, quantization, and event-triggered synchronization. We establish convergence guarantees for general non-convex objectives and show that DMFL-SQ achieves an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rate in expected squared Moreau-envelope stationarity despite sparse, quantized, and event-triggered communication. We further derive PAC-Bayes generalization guarantees for the fairness-aware mixture objective. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and the real heterogeneous MUSMET EEG dataset demonstrate that DMFL-SQ substantially reduces communication while maintaining predictive performance and improving fairness across clients. Together, our theoretical and empirical results show that personalization, fairness, and communication efficiency can be jointly achieved in decentralized learning while preserving the dominant convergence rate.

</details>


### [231] [A Single Suffix to Break Them All: Basin-Aware Jailbreaks for Merged Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26506)
*Yu Zhe,Yixin Tan,Junhao Wei,Wang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Model merging enables combining multiple fine-tuned models without additional training, but its safety implications remain poorly understood. Prior work primarily attributes merging risks to unsafe constituent models, implicitly assuming that merging individually aligned models preserves safety. In contrast, we show that model merging reveals a previously overlooked jailbreak risk rooted in the pretrained foundation model, even when all constituent models are individually safety-aligned. Motivated by this observation, we study a new threat setting where an attacker constructs jailbreak prompts that generalize across merged models sharing the same pretrained backbone, without access to the exact merging coefficients or constituent checkpoints. To exploit this phenomenon, we propose \textbf{Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)}, which formulates jailbreak generation as a min--max optimization over the merging space to produce transferable adversarial suffixes across merged model families. Experiments across diverse backbones and merging settings show that BAJ achieves consistently high transfer success rates and remains effective under existing defenses.

</details>


### [232] [Algorithmic Principles For Multiclass Learning Are Hard To Come By: Limits of Regularization and Proper Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26516)
*Julian Asilis,Shaddin Dughmi,Vatsal Sharan,Alec Sun,Shang-Hua Teng,Chang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了统计学习理论中两个基本问题：可学习性与如何学习。作者否定性地回答了三个此前悬而未决的问题：(1) 存在可学习但无法嵌入任何真适学习（properly learnable）类别的多类问题，即学习不能通过扩大假设类归约为真适学习；(2) 真适学习可能需要在训练样本上犯错，且任何子线性错误率都是必要的；(3) 正则化并非通用学习器，SRM和局部正则化器都无法覆盖所有可学习类。同时给出SRM可学习性的两个充分条件和通过揭示偏好可积性刻画SRM可表示性的正面理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习理论中，可学习性通常可由组合维度（如VC维）优雅刻画，但"如何学习"这一问题长期未解：已知的通用多类学习器依赖指数大小的一包含结构（one-inclusion structure）的复杂定向，而诸如真适学习和正则化等熟悉的算法原则仍缺乏深入理解。作者希望回答学习是否可归约为真适学习（可能在更大的假设类上），以及真适或不真适多类学习能否被合适的正则化器捕获，从而填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者通过构造反例和理论分析来回答问题。具体方法包括：(1) 构造一个可学习的多类问题，证明其无法嵌入任何真适可学习类，从而否定学习归约为真适学习的可能性；(2) 分析真适学习的训练误差，证明每个真适可学习类都存在训练误差为o(m)的真适学习器，同时证明任何给定的子线性尺度a_m=o(m)对某些问题都是必要的；(3) 构造反例证明SRM学习器和局部正则化器分别无法学习某些可学习类，并给出SRM可学习性的两个充分条件以及通过揭示偏好（revealed preferences）的可积性来刻画SRM可表示性。

Result: 主要结果是否定性的，解决了三个开放问题：第一，存在可学习的多类问题不能嵌入任何真适可学习类，因此学习不能通过扩大假设类归约为真适学习；第二，真适学习可能需要训练误差，且精确刻画了这一现象——每个真适可学习类都有o(m)误差的真适学习器，但任何子线性尺度都是某些问题所必需的；第三，正则化不是通用学习器，存在真适可学习但无法被任何SRM学习者学习的类，以及可学习但无法被任何局部正则化器学习的类。正面结果包括SRM可学习性的两个充分条件和SRM可表示性与揭示偏好可积性之间的关系。

Conclusion: 本文通过一系否定性结果揭示了学习问题的深层结构：学习不能简单地归约为真适学习，真适学习的训练误差行为具有精细的尺度依赖性，而基于正则化的学习范式（包括SRM和局部正则化）本质上无法覆盖所有可学习问题。这说明了多类学习问题的复杂性和多样性远超此前的认识。同时，SRM可表示性与揭示偏好可积性的联系为正面理论提供了方向，也为未来研究如何设计更通用的学习算法提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Two of the most fundamental questions in statistical learning theory are the following: which prediction problems are learnable, and how should they be learned? For the former, elegant answers often take the form of combinatorial dimensions. The latter question, however, has proved considerably more elusive: all known general-purpose multiclass learners rely on intricate orientations of exponentially large one-inclusion structures, and familiar algorithmic principles such as proper learning and regularization remain poorly understood. Motivated by prior work, we ask whether learning reduces to proper learning---possibly over a larger hypothesis class---and whether proper or improper multiclass learning can ultimately be captured by suitable regularizers.
  Our primary results answer both questions negatively, resolving three open problems from prior work. First, we exhibit a learnable multiclass problem that cannot be embedded in any properly learnable class, meaning learning cannot be reduced to proper learning by enlarging the hypothesis class. Second, we demonstrate that proper learning can require training error and characterize this phenomenon precisely: every properly learnable class admits a proper learner making $o(m)$ errors on samples of size $m$, but every prescribed sublinear scale $a_m=o(m)$ is necessary for some properly learnable problem. Third, regularization is not a general learner: we exhibit a properly learnable class that cannot be learned by any Structural Risk Minimization (SRM) learner, and a learnable class that cannot be learned by any local regularizer. We complement these impossibility results with a positive theory that gives two sufficient conditions for SRM learnability and characterizes SRM representability through integrability of revealed preferences.

</details>


### [233] [High Probability Derivative Bounds for Random tanh Neural Networks on a Hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26526)
*Josef Dick,Michael Feischl,Fabian Zehetgruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对宽随机神经网络，建立了混合输入导数的高概率界。对Xavier初始化的tanh网络，证明在隐藏宽度满足一定条件下，高阶混合导数的增长率从指数降为关于深度L的多项式（L^{|u|-1}），并由此得到Lipschitz常数和加权Sobolev范数的高概率界，为QMC积分和基于QMC的训练分析提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的导数正则性是理解其训练动力学和泛化能力的关键。经典的确定性分析基于权重矩阵的Euclidean算子范数，导致导数界随深度指数增长，这过于悲观。作者希望为宽随机网络建立更紧的导数界，揭示随机性带来的正则化效应。

Method: 作者将最高阶导数项中的线性部分分离出来，通过可测有限网（measurable finite nets）控制对应的切方向，从而避免了对权重矩阵Euclidean算子范数的直接依赖。对激活函数导数满足阶乘增长界的网络建立一般性高概率估计，再特化到tanh网络和Xavier初始化，推导出具体的宽度条件和概率界。

Result: 证明了对于标量输出的tanh网络，当宽度n满足n≥C(L³n₀²(1+log n₀)+L²(1+log(L/η)))时，以至少1-η的概率，|D^u R_{Φ^(L)}(x)| ≤ C₀|u|!(C₁L)^{|u|-1}∏_{j∈u}β_j(η,n₀)对所有非空u⊆[n₀]和x∈[0,1]^{n₀}一致成立。一阶导数界与深度无关，|u|阶平方自由混合导数最多以多项式L^{|u|-1}增长。由此得到Euclidean Lipschitz常数和加权Sobolev范数的高概率界。

Conclusion: 该结果表明宽随机tanh网络的导数正则性远优于确定性分析的悲观估计，且这些正则性估计可以与准蒙特卡洛（QMC）积分理论相结合，为QMC训练神经网络的收敛性分析提供了一条新的路径。

Abstract: We establish high-probability bounds for mixed input derivatives of wide random neural networks whose activation derivatives satisfy a factorial growth bound. Our main result specializes these estimates to $\tanh$ networks with Xavier initialization. A direct deterministic analysis based on Euclidean operator norms of the weight matrices yields derivative bounds that generally grow exponentially with the depth. We show that this growth can be substantially improved for sufficiently wide Gaussian networks by isolating the term that is linear in the highest-order derivative and controlling the corresponding tangent directions by measurable finite nets.
  For scalar-output $\tanh$ networks with Gaussian weights and Xavier initialization, we prove that there exist constants $C,C_0,C_1>0$ such that, whenever the common hidden width satisfies $n \geq C\left(L^3n_0^2(1+\log n_0)+L^2\left(1+\log(L/η)\right)\right)$, then, with probability at least $1-η$, the estimate $\left|D^u\mathcal{R}_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)\right| \leq C_0 |u|! (C_1L)^{|u|-1}\prod_{j\in u}β_j(η,n_0)$ holds simultaneously for every non-empty $u\subseteq[n_0]$ and every $x\in[0,1]^{n_0}$. Thus, the first-order derivative bound is independent of the depth, while a square-free mixed derivative of order $|u|$ grows at most polynomially as $L^{|u|-1}$, apart from the coordinate factors. As consequences, we obtain high-probability bounds for the Euclidean Lipschitz constant and for weighted Sobolev norms of the network realization. The latter connect the derivative estimates to quasi-Monte Carlo integration and indicate how such regularity can enter the analysis of QMC-based training.

</details>


### [234] [Predicting Quantifiability from Primary Screens to Prioritize Dose-Response Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26538)
*Sean Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将"可量化性"（quantifiability）作为区别于生物学活性的独立分流目标的建模框架。研究发现，可量化性可以从前期的低成本筛选中被强预测，且响应特征比分子结构更具预测力，该预测在未见过的化学骨架和不同的检测机制家族中均表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高通量药物筛选策略假设"确认生物活性的化合物必然能产生可用的效价估计"，但实际上具有生物活性的化合物仍可能无法产生可报告的剂量-响应估计。因此需要将可量化性作为独立于生物学活性的分流目标来建模，以优化昂贵的剂量-响应分析资源的分配。

Method: 提出一个用于建模可量化性的框架，将其作为区别于生物学活性的独立分流标准。利用前期低成本筛选的特征预测随访测试能否产生可用的效价估计，并评估响应特征与分子结构各自的信息贡献，同时在不同化学骨架和检测机制家族上进行泛化验证。

Result: 可量化性可以从前期的低成本筛选中被强预测；大多数预测信息来自观察到的筛选特征而非分子结构；基于响应的预测在未见过的化学骨架和held-out检测机制家族上表现稳健；成功量化的概率随响应幅度和检测背景变化显著。

Conclusion: 实验可测量性（区别于生物学活性）是筛选结果的一个可预测属性，可量化性感知的分流策略能够改善昂贵的剂量-响应分析资源的分配效率。

Abstract: High-throughput drug screening relies on low-cost primary assays to prioritize compounds for more expensive dose-response profiling, where potency is ultimately quantified. Current screening strategies largely focus on identifying compounds that will confirm biological activity on follow-up, implicitly assuming that confirmed activity will also yield a usable potency estimate. However, confirmed biological activity in screening does not necessarily translate into a quantifiable potency, because active compounds can still fail to produce a reportable dose-response estimate. We therefore present a framework for modeling quantifiability, whether follow-up testing will yield a usable potency estimate, as a distinct triage objective from biological activity. Quantifiability was strongly predictable from the preceding low-cost screen, with most predictive information arising from the observed screening features rather than molecular structure. Response-based predictors remained robust on previously unseen chemical scaffolds and generalized across held-out assay-mechanism families, while the probability of successful quantification varied strongly with response amplitude and assay context. These findings establish experimental measurability, distinct from biological activity, as a predictable property of screening outcomes and show that quantifiability-aware triage can improve the allocation of costly dose-response profiling capacity.

</details>


### [235] [Chart2SVG: Editable SVG Generation from Raster Chart Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26544)
*Jinning Cui,Lu Chen,Haoyan Shi,Yue He,Chenglong Wang,Mengyu Zhou,Weidong Huang,Yunhai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chart2SVG 是一个多模态大语言模型，可将静态栅格图表转换为结构化、语义丰富的 SVG 格式，支持程序化编辑，并通过专门的训练目标和渲染感知的后训练阶段实现高保真度的结构恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态栅格图表缺乏可编辑性和结构信息，难以被程序化修改和复用；现有的图表理解方法通常只输出最终答案或简单描述，无法支持深层次的交互式操作和图表重用。

Method: 1) 将图表特定语义 token 融入视觉-语言模型，同时捕捉几何基元及其功能角色；2) 构建 Beagle+ 数据集（33K 标准化和结构蒸馏样本）以支持鲁棒的结构恢复；3) 结合专门训练目标与渲染感知的后训练阶段；4) 构建 Chart Structure Graph (CSG) 以暴露视觉依赖关系。

Result: 实验表明 Chart2SVG 在重建保真度和下游编辑实用性方面显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: Chart2SVG 有效推进了智能交互式可视化工具的发展，实现了交互式探索、图表重新定位和布局复用等多种高级操作。

Abstract: We present Chart2SVG, a multimodal large language model that converts static raster charts into structurally organized, semantically enriched SVGs that support programmatic editing. By incorporating chart-specific semantic tokens into a vision-language model, Chart2SVG captures both geometric primitives and their functional roles. To support robust structural recovery, we introduce Beagle+, a dataset of 33K canonicalized and structurally distilled chart samples. Our approach combines specialized training objectives with a rendering-aware post-training phase, producing SVGs that are both visually accurate and structurally consistent. To facilitate higher-level manipulations, we construct a Chart Structure Graph (CSG) that exposes visual dependencies, enabling tasks such as interactive exploration, chart repurposing, and layout reuse. Experiments show that Chart2SVG substantially outperforms baselines in reconstruction fidelity and downstream editing utility, advancing the development of intelligent and interactive visualization tools.

</details>


### [236] [Arrive and Survive: Scaling Safe Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning from One-Bit Failure Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26571)
*Guopeng Li,Yiyang Duan,Yiru Jiao,Chengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Safe-CRL框架，针对失败终止型马尔可夫决策过程中对比强化学习（CRL）因忽略失败终止移除的概率质量而产生的目标达成价值高估偏差问题，引入质量加权InfoNCE和对数生存质量得分两项最小化修正，仅利用失败终止的一比特信号即可实现安全的目标条件策略学习，在十二个失败易发的机器人导航与移动任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在失败终止的MDP中，现有CRL构建正样本时仅考虑失败前的未来目标，未纳入失败终止所移除的概率质量。理论分析表明这会导致目标达成价值的系统性高估偏差，使接近失败的轨迹因残存少量未来占用却被赋予过强的成功监督信号，从而强化不安全动作，最终导致策略学习失败和不可持续的goal-reaching行为。

Method: 提出两项最小化修正：(1) 质量加权InfoNCE（mass-weighted InfoNCE），在critic学习中纠正短生存未来轨迹的过度加权问题；(2) 对数生存质量得分（log-survival-mass score），在策略优化中恢复缺失的生存质量项。整个方法Safe-CRL仅依赖失败终止提供的一比特信号，无需额外监督。

Result: 在十二个失败易发的机器人导航和移动任务中，Safe-CRL一致性地提升了生存率，并在goal-reaching性能上大幅超越Scaling-CRL基线；深层Safe-CRL策略还展现出复杂的避障规避行为。

Conclusion: 本研究补全了失败终止条件下的CRL理论，并提供了一个可扩展的安全强化学习框架，将安全约束无缝融入现有的对比式目标条件策略学习方法之中。代码已开源。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) scales effectively in goal-conditioned tasks by casting policy learning into a self-supervised contrastive objective. However, in a failure-terminated Markov decision process, established CRL considers pre-failure future goals only when constructing positive samples, without accounting for the probability mass removed by failure termination. Our theoretical analysis shows that this omission induces a systematic overestimation bias in goal-reaching values. Consequently, near-failure trajectories provide disproportionately strong supervision of success despite retaining little future occupancy. Unsafe actions can thereby be reinforced through catastrophic failure bootstrapping, leading to failed policy learning and unsustainable goal-reaching behaviours. To address this problem, we introduce two minimal yet strong corrections: mass-weighted InfoNCE corrects the overweighting of short surviving futures in critic learning, and a log-survival-mass score restores the missing survival mass in policy optimization. The resulting method, Safe Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (Safe-CRL), requires only the one-bit signal provided by failure termination to scale safe goal-conditioned policy learning. Across twelve failure-prone robot navigation and locomotion tasks, Safe-CRL consistently improves survival and substantially outperforms the Scaling-CRL baseline in goal-reaching performance. Additionally, deep Safe-CRL policies exhibit complex failure-avoidance behaviours. This study completes the CRL theory under failure termination and provides a scalable safe RL framework. The code is available via https://github.com/RomainLITUD/safe-crl.

</details>


### [237] [Activation Outliers Matter: Robust Recovery for Quantized Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26581)
*Tanzila Rahman,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Zhuang Ma,Anandharaju Durai Raju,Yao Wang,Xing Huang,Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Yonghan Dong,Wei Guo,Yaoyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了MXFP8、MXFP4和HiF4等低比特量化方案在多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的表现，发现MXFP8接近无损而4比特量化性能显著下降，并通过消融实验确定激活量化是性能损失的主要来源。基于此，提出Residual Fallback Quantization（RFQ）轻量级激活重建框架，通过辅助量化残差路径补偿量化误差，在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL上有效恢复MXFP4和HiF4量化下的性能损失，缩小与BF16基线的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化是降低MLLMs计算和内存开销的重要途径，但4比特量化会导致显著性能退化。现有研究缺乏对量化方案和误差来源的系统性理解，需要明确性能瓶颈所在并开发有效的缓解策略。

Method: 首先对MXFP8、MXFP4和HiF4等格式在视频生成和推理任务的多类MLLMs上进行系统性基准测试与消融分析，定位激活量化为主要性能瓶颈。随后提出RFQ框架：在主超低比特激活表示之外增加一个辅助量化残差路径，显式建模并补偿量化误差，无需架构修改且计算开销可忽略。

Result: 实验表明MXFP8接近无损，而MXFP4和HiF4等4比特量化导致显著退化；RFQ在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL上一致地恢复MXFP4和HiF4量化下的部分性能损失，显著缩小了与BF16基线的差距，覆盖视频生成和4个推理基准。

Conclusion: 激活量化是超低比特MLLMs性能退化的主导瓶颈，基于残差的激活重建（RFQ）是一种有效且实用的4比特部署策略，在保持效率优势的同时显著提升量化模型的性能保真度。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization offers a promising avenue for reducing the computational and memory demands of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Recent hardware support for low-precision formats, ranging from MXFP8 to ultra-low-bit formats such as MXFP4 and HiF4, has accelerated research into efficient MLLM training and deployment. In this work, we present a systematic study of these quantization schemes in representative MLLMs that span both video generation and reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that MXFP8 achieves near-lossless performance, whereas aggressive 4-bit quantization leads to significant degradation. Through extensive ablations, we identify activation quantization as the primary source of this performance loss, contributing substantially more than weight quantization. Motivated by this observation, we propose Residual Fallback Quantization (RFQ), a lightweight activation reconstruction framework that supplements the primary ulta-low-bit activation representation with an auxiliary quantized residual pathway. By explicitly modeling and compensating for quantization errors, RFQ improves activation fidelity while preserving the efficiency advantages of ultra-low-bit computation. RFQ requires no architectural modifications and incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments on Wan2.2 and Qwen3-VL demonstrate that RFQ consistently recovers a substantial portion of the performance lost under the quantization of MXFP4 and HiF4, significantly narrowing the gap to BF16 baselines across both generation and 4 reasoning benchmarks. Our findings establish activation quantization as the dominant bottleneck in ultra-low-bit MLLMs and highlight residual-based activation reconstruction as an effective and practical strategy for robust 4-bit deployment.

</details>


### [238] [J-Zero: Unified Challenger--Solver--Judge Co-Evolution from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26582)
*Gyouk Chu,Myeongho Jeon,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: J-Zero提出了一种统一的"挑战者-求解器-裁判"三方协同进化框架，支持语言模型在可验证和不可验证领域中的自我进化。通过对抗交互生成难度递增的任务，并利用已知顺序的偏好对训练裁判，J-Zero在可验证领域平均提升4.2分、不可验证领域提升8.0分，且可持续改进至少十轮迭代，优于两轮即退化的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化语言模型有望降低人工监督成本，但现有方法主要集中在可验证领域，不可验证领域的自我进化研究相对匮乏。需要一个统一的框架来支持跨领域的自我改进。

Method: 提出Challenger-Solver-Judge三方协同进化框架（J-Zero）。Challenger生成逐渐困难的任务，Solver学习产生更高质量的答案，二者通过对抗交互共同进化。Judge的适应不使用自身评分，而是利用事先已知顺序的偏好对：即Solver的答案优于Challenger的答案，以及Solver的分解重组合答案优于一次性答案。

Result: 在可验证领域平均优于基线4.2分，在不可验证领域平均优于基线8.0分。至少可持续改进十次迭代，而基线方法在两次迭代后即出现性能退化。

Conclusion: J-Zero通过统一的三方协同进化框架有效解决了可验证与不可验证领域中的自我进化问题，其"零数据"的裁判适应策略避免了依赖自身评分可能导致的偏差，展现了更强的持续改进能力和更优的性能表现。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models have recently emerged as a promising path toward superintelligence, with the advantage of reducing the cost of human supervision. While considerable progress has been made in verifiable domains, self-evolution in unverifiable domains remains substantially less explored. We propose Judge co-adaptation from Zero data (J-Zero), a unified Challenger--Solver--Judge co-evolution framework that supports self-improvement across both domains. The Challenger and Solver co-evolve through an adversarial interaction: the Challenger generates increasingly difficult tasks, while the Solver learns to produce higher-quality responses to them.
